Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the homotopy object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 583. The moduli space has dimension at most 534 with 3 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the homotopy decomposition. By minimality, the element a^583 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 534-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
